{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787308235626,"sessionID":"ses_fdc228d6fffebBqaV0qDEp8SSv","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-3c82e47e-11a3-4953-b091-fc76a60430f3-32","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","limit":80},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # Power Choice Pro — terminology dictionary\n2: \n3: Canonical **user-facing** language for the product. Use this for UI copy, empty states, emails, and OpenAPI/field *descriptions*.\n4: \n5: **Scope**\n6: \n7: - Apply consistently in the SPA and human-readable API docs.\n8: - Do **not** rename database columns, JSON field names, path segments, or enums unless a change is local and very low risk.\n9: - Internal code may keep `alternative`, `criteria`, `observation`, `probe`, `vote` as implementation vocabulary.\n10: \n11: **Related product nouns (out of this glossary)**\n12: \n13: - **Application** / API keys — toolkit integrations, not decision projects.\n14: - **Workspace** — company/account context (signup), not a project.\n15: \n16: ---\n17: \n18: ## Principles\n19: \n20: 1. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.\n21: 2. Everyday English first; avoid MCDA jargon in the main UI.\n22: 3. Verbs for actions, nouns for things: *compare options using factors*.\n23: 4. Brand-safe: “choice” is product flavor (Power Choice), not the name of an option or mode.\n24: 5. Top-level work unit: **Project**. Primary action: **Compare** / **comparison**.\n25: \n26: ---\n27: \n28: ## Canonical terms\n29: \n30: ### 1. Container & framing\n31: \n32: | Role | Canonical | Notes |\n33: |------|-----------|--------|\n34: | Owned work unit | **Project** | Nav, lists, CRUD, routes |\n35: | What it’s about | **decision** | Framing only (“Structure the decision”) — not a second entity |\n36: | Account context | **Workspace** | Company/signup only |\n37: \n38: ### 2. Project mode\n39: \n40: | Role | Canonical | Internal |\n41: |------|-----------|----------|\n42: | Single winner | **Pick one** | `project_exclusive_mode = true` |\n43: | Full ordering | **Rank all** | `project_exclusive_mode = false` |\n44: | Edit labels | **Choose one winner** / **Rank all options** | OK long form on edit |\n45: \n46: Avoid in UI: Choice, Prioritization, exclusive, selection-mode.\n47: \n48: ### 3. Things being compared\n49: \n50: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n51: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n52: | Entity | **Option** | `alternative_*`, `CustomerProjectAlternatives` |\n53: | Count / empty states | **options** | — |\n54: \n55: Avoid in UI: alternative, choice (as the item), item.\n56: \n57: ### 4. Evaluation dimensions\n58: \n59: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n60: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n61: | Entity | **Factor** | `factor_*` |\n62: | Section | **Factors** | — |\n63: | No separate factors | **Overall only** | Synthetic factor title: **Overall** |\n64: | Multi | **Multiple factors** | — |\n65: | Templates | **Template** | Project / options / factors scopes; internal: `CustomerFactorTemplates`, `project_templates.yml` |\n66: \n67: Avoid in UI: criteria, criterion, rubric (except rare help metaphor).\n68: \n69: Observation type `criteria` means factor-vs-factor comparisons — never say “criteria” to users.\n70: \n71: ### 5. Comparing (giving input)\n72: \n73: | Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n74: |------|-----------|-----------------|\n75: | Verb / CTA | **Compare** / **Start comparing** | vote APIs, probe routes |\n76: | One unit | **comparison** | `observation`, probe |\n77: | Progress | **comparisons** | — |\n78: | Finish | **Mark complete** | — |\n79: | Locked | **Input closed** / **Comparisons locked** | `disabled` |\n80: | Status collecting | **Collecting input** | — |\n\n(Showing lines 1-80 of 226. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.","truncated":true,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","text":"# Power Choice Pro — terminology dictionary\n\nCanonical **user-facing** language for the product. Use this for UI copy, empty states, emails, and OpenAPI/field *descriptions*.\n\n**Scope**\n\n- Apply consistently in the SPA and human-readable API docs.\n- Do **not** rename database columns, JSON field names, path segments, or enums unless a change is local and very low risk.\n- Internal code may keep `alternative`, `criteria`, `observation`, `probe`, `vote` as implementation vocabulary.\n\n**Related product nouns (out of this glossary)**\n\n- **Application** / API keys — toolkit integrations, not decision projects.\n- **Workspace** — company/account context (signup), not a project.\n\n---\n\n## Principles\n\n1. One primary noun per concept in user-facing copy.\n2. Everyday English first; avoid MCDA jargon in the main UI.\n3. Verbs for actions, nouns for things: *compare options using factors*.\n4. Brand-safe: “choice” is product flavor (Power Choice), not the name of an option or mode.\n5. Top-level work unit: **Project**. Primary action: **Compare** / **comparison**.\n\n---\n\n## Canonical terms\n\n### 1. Container & framing\n\n| Role | Canonical | Notes |\n|------|-----------|--------|\n| Owned work unit | **Project** | Nav, lists, CRUD, routes |\n| What it’s about | **decision** | Framing only (“Structure the decision”) — not a second entity |\n| Account context | **Workspace** | Company/signup only |\n\n### 2. Project mode\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal |\n|------|-----------|----------|\n| Single winner | **Pick one** | `project_exclusive_mode = true` |\n| Full ordering | **Rank all** | `project_exclusive_mode = false` |\n| Edit labels | **Choose one winner** / **Rank all options** | OK long form on edit |\n\nAvoid in UI: Choice, Prioritization, exclusive, selection-mode.\n\n### 3. Things being compared\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Entity | **Option** | `alternative_*`, `CustomerProjectAlternatives` |\n| Count / empty states | **options** | — |\n\nAvoid in UI: alternative, choice (as the item), item.\n\n### 4. Evaluation dimensions\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Entity | **Factor** | `factor_*` |\n| Section | **Factors** | — |\n| No separate factors | **Overall only** | Synthetic factor title: **Overall** |\n| Multi | **Multiple factors** | — |\n| Templates | **Template** | Project / options / factors scopes; internal: `CustomerFactorTemplates`, `project_templates.yml` |\n\nAvoid in UI: criteria, criterion, rubric (except rare help metaphor).\n\nObservation type `criteria` means factor-vs-factor comparisons — never say “criteria” to users.\n\n### 5. Comparing (giving input)\n\n| Role | Canonical | Internal (keep) |\n|------|-----------|-----------------|\n| Verb / CTA | **Compare** / **Start comparing** | vote APIs, probe routes |\n| One unit | **comparison** | `observation`, probe |\n| Progress | **comparisons** | — |\n| Finish | **Mark complete** | — |\n| Locked | **Input closed** / **Comparisons locked** | `disabled` |\n| Status collecting | **Collecting input** | — |","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":80,"totalLines":226,"truncated":true}},"title":"docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md","time":{"start":1787308234389,"end":1787308235601}},"id":"prt_023df5631001YRaEHjS63EWuA1","sessionID":"ses_fdc228d6fffebBqaV0qDEp8SSv","messageID":"msg_023df0e350010ENf1Gq8w1H7p1"}}

Never compares different factors to each other.\n    \"\"\"\n    gs = _groups(groups)\n    by_key: dict[tuple, list[dict[int, float]]] = defaultdict(list)\n    for g in gs:\n        gt = _group_type(g)\n        cid = g.get(\"criterion_id\")\n        key = (gt, None if cid is None else int(cid))\n        order = effective_group_rank_order(g)\n        if order:\n            by_key[key].append(_normalize_dist(rank_order_to_map(order)))\n    divergences: list[float] = []\n    for maps in by_key.values():\n        if len(maps) < 2:\n            continue\n        for i in range(len(maps)):\n            for j in range(i + 1, len(maps)):\n                divergences.append(js_divergence(maps[i], maps[j]))\n    if not divergences:\n        return 0.0\n    mean_js = sum(divergences) / len(divergences)\n    return float(max(0.0, min(1.0, 1.0 - mean_js / _LN2)))\n\n\n# Back-compat alias\ndef within_participant_stability(groups: list | None) -> float:\n    return multi_pass_consistency(groups)\n\n\ndef multi_pass_consistency_by_channel(groups: list | None) -> dict[tuple, float]:\n    \"\"\"Per-channel multi-pass agreement. 0 : runner.inGroupDone.value))\nconst groupTotalCount = computed(() => {\n  if (showGroupIntro.value && pendingGroup.value) {\n    return Number(pendingGroup.value.question_budget || pendingGroup.value.estimated_comparisons || 0)\n  }\n  return runner.inGroupTotal.value || 0\n})\nconst groupDoneLabel = computed(() => `${groupDoneCount.value} / ${groupTotalCount.value || '—'}`)\nconst inGroupPct = computed(() => {\n  const t = groupTotalCount.value || 0\n  if (!t) return 0\n  return Math.min(100, Math.round((100 * groupDoneCount.value) / t))\n})\n\nconst comparisonsDone = computed(() => {\n  return Number(progress.value.participant_comparisons_done || 0) + (showGroupIntro.value ? {"type":"text","timestamp":1787308346764,"sessionID":"ses_fdc228d6fffebBqaV0qDEp8SSv","part":{"id":"prt_023e0bef0001StNtbLD55znGvX","messageID":"msg_023e06062001pfK5W4gGN4GMy1","sessionID":"ses_fdc228d6fffebBqaV0qDEp8SSv","type":"text","text":"# Add “Current Ranking Confidence Achieved” bar\n\nDisplay-only. Live, personal ranking quality from **all option and factor comparisons so far**. Do not change pair scheduling, Results metrics, or stop-at-threshold behavior.\n\nToday: `probe.vue` and `ShareVoteSession.vue` already show **This group** and **Overall** (count vs min passes). Share complete-view uses `metricLabel()` on server `submitter_summary.confidence` (multi-pass only). Project Confidence in `vote_ranking.py` is multi-participant — **not** this bar. Client only holds the current group plus same-channel `prior_pairings`.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Implementation steps\n\n1. **Add `client/utils/rankingConfidence.ts`** (pure, tested)\n   - Input: every channel’s pairings (option-under-factor + factor-vs-factor if ≥2 factors), item ids, ranking target.\n   - Output: `{ value: 0–1, level, label, color }`.\n   - **Score (recommended):** equal-weight mean of per-channel scores:\n     - **Coverage ~70%** — decisive pairs (`winner`/`tie`) ÷ pairs needed for that channel’s target (`question_budget` / connected ranking). `unsure`/`skip` do not add coverage.\n     - **Consistency ~20%** — repeated-pair agreement, else same-channel multi-pass JS agreement when ≥2 rank orders exist; **omit** this term on first pass (don’t force None).\n     - **Decisiveness ~10%** — top-vs-next gap from existing `fitProvisionalScores()` in `pairScheduler.ts`.\n   - Missing factor ranking (multi-factor) keeps the mean from reaching Strong.\n   - Map to six levels: **None / Low / Moderate / Fair / Good / Strong** (None at 0; then ~20% bands). Do **not** reuse or change `metricLabel()` (Sparse/Early/Building/Solid/Strong on Results).\n   - Color: interpolate `--pc-danger` → `--pc-warning` → `--pc-success` from `value`.\n\n2. **Hydrate full ranking set**\n   - Small session accumulator (composable or runner-adjacent): completed groups this visit + current `runner.answers` (update on answer/undo).\n   - Add compact `progress.ranking_evidence` on next-group / partial-flush payloads (`vote_sort_session.py` + vote/share APIs): `{ group_type, criterion_id, pass_index, item_ids, pairings }[]` for this participant.\n   - Resume/reload: seed accumulator from that snapshot so confidence isn’t session-memory-only.\n   - Do not recompute project Confidence or change ranking math.\n\n3. **UI: `RankingConfidenceBar.vue`**\n   - Persistent strip **above** the existing This group / Overall cards (both comparison surfaces).\n   - Caption **Current ranking confidence** + current level on the right; fill + tick labels (or ends + current).\n   - Custom fill (not a third identical `soft-card` primary/secondary bar). `prefers-reduced-motion`: no width/color animation.\n   - Visible during active compare, group intro, and pause. Hide on access gate and thank-you (share complete already shows a different confidence line).\n\n4. **Wire** `probe.vue` and `ShareVoteSession.vue` to the same component/util. Leave Overall/This group as-is.\n\n5. **Tests:** `client/utils/rankingConfidence.test.ts` (node:test, same pattern as `ranking.test.ts`) — empty→None; options-only vs options+factors; unsure doesn’t inflate; undo drops score; six-level boundaries. Light API test that `ranking_evidence` is present and scoped to the participant.\n\n6. **Copy/docs:** comparison UI only. Do not fold Results Agreement/Repeatability/Stability into this number (`terminology_dictionary.md`). Optional one-line note in `algos.md` that session ranking confidence is display-only and separate from Results Confidence.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Clarifying questions\n\n1. Confirm this is **this participant’s** ranking quality, not project-level Results Confidence.\n2. Show on **both** logged-in Compare (`probe.vue`) and share Compare (`ShareVoteSession.vue`)?\n3. Keep the six labels as specified, even though Results uses Sparse/Early/Building/Solid/Strong?\n4. Should **unsure/skip** only fail to raise the bar, or actively lower it?\n5. Keep the bar on pause / group intro / “maximum comparisons” screens?\n6. Any required formula, or is the coverage-led blend below acceptable?\n\n---\n\n## 3. Options, trade-offs, recommendation\n\n| | Approach | Pros | Cons |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| **A** | Reuse Overall % (comparisons / min target) | Trivial | Duplicate bar; not ranking quality; can’t later gate stop |\n| **B** | Server participant `confidence` (multi-pass) | Already exists | Stays ~0 until pass 2; ignores in-group answers; not factor+option coverage |\n| **C** | Client coverage+consistency+decisiveness over **all** option and factor channels; server sends evidence snapshot | Matches brief; live per answer; reusable for a future stop threshold | New util + small payload |\n| **D** | Server score on every answer | Single formula in Python | Chatty; bar lags without a request per click |\n\n**Recommend C.** Placement: full-width meter above the two existing cards, **label + fill** (not ticks-only). Formula: coverage-led blend so the bar moves from the first decisive answers, but Strong requires both option and factor rankings (when factors exist) plus some consistency/decisiveness — so it is not a second progress counter.\n\nOut of scope: stop-when-strong prompts, changing `project_metrics` / `metricLabel`, changing pair selection.","time":{"start":1787308326640,"end":1787308346715}}}
